    established “Runxin Health Huts” at the China Resources

    insurance customers. To extend home-based services, we
    explored the “neighborhood doctor” initiative. Services such
    as “internet + home care” have been launched in eight regions
    including Fushun Mining Bureau General Hospital of Liaoning

    influence. In 2025, China Resources Medical ranked 24th on
    the “China Top 100 ESG Pioneers” list, continuing to build the
    “Runxin” service brand.

     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
     管理層討論與分析

     BUSINESS REVIEW                                                          業務回顧
     In 2025, the medical reform continued to advance, and reforms            2025 年 醫 療 改 革 持 續 推 進，醫 保 支 付 方 式
     to medical insurance payment methods had been further                    改革不斷深化。本公司積極應對外部環境變
     deepened. In active response to changes in the external                  化，以客戶為中心，綜合醫療實現量的合理
     environment and under a customer-centric principle, the                  增長和質的有效提升，智慧健康服務開展全
     Company promoted the general healthcare services to achieve              週期服務模式探索，持續打造華潤特色醫療
     reasonable growth in scale and effective improvement in                  運營模式，提高國企辦醫核心競爭力。
     quality. It also explored full lifecycle service models in intelligent
     healthcare services, continuously developed a distinctive China
     Resources-style medical operation model, and enhanced the
     core competitiveness of state-owned healthcare providers.

     Future Outlook                                                         未來展望
     During the “15th Five-Year Plan” period, in response to the 「十五五」時期，為不斷滿足人民日益增長的
     growing health needs of the people, the healthcare industry 健康需求，醫療行業正在從「以治病為中心」
     is shifting from a “focus on treatment” to a “focus on health” 向「以健康為中心」轉變。綜合政策、客戶、
     approach. Based on a comprehensive analysis of policies, 市場趨勢變化，醫療健康行業的機會大於挑
     customers, and the market dynamics, the healthcare sector         戰，是具備長期增長潛力的朝陽產業。賽道
     presents more opportunities than challenges, and is a sunrise     選擇上，華潤醫療將以客戶為中心，推動傳
     industry with long-term growth potential. In terms of strategic 統綜合醫療服務轉型升級，培育發展智慧健
     focus, China Resources Medical will take a customer-centric 康服務業務，佈局衍生健康產品業務，加快
     approach to drive the transformation and the upgrade of           實現從「醫療」到「健康」的轉型。
     traditional integrated medical services, foster the development
     of intelligent healthcare services, expand into derivative health
     products businesses, and accelerate the transition from
     “medical care” to “healthcare”.

     In terms of general healthcare services, the Company will              在綜合醫療業務方面，本公司將積極提升在
     actively enhance its functions and role within the regional            區域醫療衛生體系中的功能和作用，深化成

Dividend Policy                                                     股息政策
According to the dividend policy of the Group, subject to           本集團之股息政策，乃在本集團盈利且不影
compliance with applicable rules and regulations (including         響本集團正常經營的情況下，遵守適用的規
Cayman Islands laws) and the Articles of Association, the           則與規定（包括開曼群島法律）與組織章程細
Company will pay dividend to the Shareholders when the Group        則由本公司向股東宣派股息。本公司擬以年
recorded net profit during the period and payment of dividend       度股息形式與股東分享溢利，派息後的利潤
would not affect its normal operation. The Company intends          餘額將用於本集團之發展與經營。
to share its profits with Shareholders in the form of annual
dividend and the remaining net profits will be used for the
Group’s development and operations.

The Company’s ability to pay dividends will depend upon,            本公司派發股息的能力取決於（其中包括）本
among other things, the general financial condition of the Group,   集團之一般財務狀況、現時與未來之經營、
the Group’s current and future operations, liquidity position and   流動資金狀況、資本需求以及自本公司之附
capital requirement of the Group as well as dividends received      屬公司收取之股息。本公司派發股息亦同時
from the Company’s subsidiaries. The payment of the dividend        受限於開曼群島法律與組織章程細則之任何
by the Company is also subject to any restrictions under the        限制所規限。
Cayman Islands laws and the Articles of Association.

The dividend policy will continue to be reviewed from time to       有關股息政策仍會不時檢討，不保證會在任
time and there can be no assurance that dividends will be paid      何指定期間派付任何特定金額的股息。
in any particular amount for any given period.

the Company, and CR Pharmaceutical and the Company are           公司為彼此之關連人士，故根據上市規則第
connected persons of each other. Accordingly, the transactions   14A 章，2026/2028 合 作 框 架 協 議 項 下 擬 進
contemplated under the Cooperation Framework Agreement           行之交易構成持續關連交易。

adopted and makes judgements and estimates that are                  斷和估計。

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China 我們已審計列載於第 119 至 251 頁的華潤醫

or loss, the consolidated statement of comprehensive income, 合全面收益報表、綜合權益變動報表和綜合

and notes, comprising material accounting policy information
and other explanatory information.

Basis for opinion                                                  意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong 我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審
Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the Hong Kong 計準則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our 的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務

the Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated 據適用於公眾利益實體財務報表審計的香港

for Professional Accountants (the “Code”), as applicable to 已履行這些規定及守則中的其他專業道德責

      2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (continued)                                   2.1 編製基準（續）
           As at 31 December 2025, the Group’s current liabilities             於 2025 年 12 月 31 日，本 集 團 的 流 動
           exceeded its current assets by RMB1.58 billion. As                  負 債 較 其 流 動 資 產 高 出 人 民 幣 15.8 億
           at 31 December 2025, the Group has unutilised bank                  元。於 2025 年 12 月 31 日，本 集 團 擁 有
           facilities of approximately HKD3.18 billion and RMB4.17             未 動 用 銀 行 授 信 額 度 約 31.8 億 港 元 及
           billion (equivalent to approximately RMB7.04 billion in             人民幣 41.7 億元（折合共約人民幣 70.4
           total). The directors of the Company have reviewed the              億元）。本公司董事已審閱本集團未來
           Group’s cash flow forecast for the next twelve months               十 二 個 月 的 現 金 流 量 預 測，並 認 為 本

           to direct the relevant activities of the investee).                 達致控制權。

           or indirectly, less than a majority of the voting or similar        對象少於大多數的投票權或類似權利，
           rights of an investee, the Group considers all relevant             本集團於評估其對投資對象是否擁有

           (c) Fair value measurement                                          (c) 公允價值計量
                The Group measures its financial assets at FVTPL                 本集團於各報告期末按公允價值
                at fair value at the end of each reporting period.               計量以公允價值計量且其變動計
                Fair value is the price that would be received to sell           入 損 益 的 金 融 資 產。公 允 價 值 乃
                an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly           在市場參與者於計量日期進行的
                transaction between market participants at the                   有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉
                measurement date. The fair value measurement                     移 負 債 所 支 付 的 價 格。公 允 價 值
                is based on the presumption that the transaction                 計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉移
                to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place          負債的交易於資產或負債的主要
                either in the principal market for the asset or liability,       市場進行或於未有主要市場的情
                or in the absence of a principal market, in the most             況 下，則 於 資 產 或 負 債 的 最 有 利
                advantageous market for the asset or liability. The              市 場 進 行。主 要 或 最 有 利 市 場 須
                principal or the most advantageous market must be                位 於 本 集 團 能 到 達 的 地 方。資 產
                accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset              或負債的公允價值乃基於市場參
                or a liability is measured using the assumptions that            與者為資產或負債定價所用的假
                market participants would use when pricing the                   設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼等
                asset or liability, assuming that market participants            的最佳經濟利益行事）。
                act in their economic best interest.

                General approach                                              一般方法

                ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 分 兩 個 階 段 確 認。
                exposures for which there has not been a significant          就自首次確認起未有顯著增加的
                increase in credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs       信貸風險而言，須為未來 12 個月
                are provided for credit losses that result from default       內可能發生違約事件而導致的信
                events that are possible within the next 12 months (a         貸虧損（12 個月預期信貸虧損）計
                12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which           提 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。就 自 初 始 確 認
                there has been a significant increase in credit risk          起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而言，
                since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required       不 論 何 時 發 生 違 約，於 餘 下 風 險
                for credit losses expected over the remaining life            年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計提
                of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the            虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。
                default (a lifetime ECL).

                At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether            於 各 報 告 日 期，本 集 團 評 估 金 融
                the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased       工具的信貸風險自首次確認以來
                significantly since initial recognition. When making          是 否 大 幅 增 加。進 行 評 估 時，本
                the assessment, the Group considers reasonable                集團會考慮合理及有理據且毋須
                and supportable information that is available without         花費過度成本或精力即可獲得的
                undue cost or effort, including both quantitative             資 料，包 括 以 本 集 團 過 往 的 經 驗
                and qualitative information and analysis, based on            及有依據的信用評估（包括前瞻性
                the Group’s historical experience and informed                信息）為基礎的定量和定性資料及
                credit assessment, that includes forward-looking              分析。
                information.

                The Group considers a financial asset in default              倘合約付款已逾期 90 日，則本集
                when contractual payments are 90 days past due.               團 認 為 金 融 資 產 違 約。然 而，在
                However, in certain cases, the Group may also                 若 干 情 況 下，倘 內 部 或 外 部 資 料
                consider a financial asset to be in default when              顯 示，在 沒 有 計 及 本 集 團 所 持 有
                internal or external information indicates that               的 任 何 信 貸 提 升 措 施 前，本 集 團
                the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding              不大可能悉數收到未償還合約款
                contractual amounts in full before taking into account        項，則 本 集 團 亦 可 認 為 金 融 資 產
                any credit enhancements held by the Group. A                  違 約。倘 無 法 合 理 預 期 收 回 合 約
                financial asset is written off when there is no               現金流量，則撇銷金融資產。

       Own equity instruments which are reacquired and                   由本公司或本集團重新購入而持
       held by the Company or the Group (treasury shares)                有之自有權益工具（庫存股份）乃
       are recognised directly in equity at cost. No gain or             按 成 本 直 接 在 權 益 中 確 認。本 集
       loss is recognised in the statement of profit or loss             團就自有權益工具之購買、出售、
       on the purchase, sale, issue or cancellation of the               發 行 或 註 銷，概 無 於 損 益 表 確 認
       Group’s own equity instruments.                                   任何收益或虧損。

   (p) Inventories                                                  (p) 存貨
       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net               存貨按成本與可變現淨值之較低
       realisable value. Cost is determined on the first-                者 列 賬。存 貨 成 本 採 用 先 進 先 出
       in, first-out basis. Net realisable value is based on             法 釐 定。可 變 現 淨 值 則 按 估 計 售
       estimated selling prices less any estimated costs to              價減於出售時將產生的任何估計
       be incurred to disposal.                                          成本計算。

   (q) Cash and cash equivalents                                    (q) 現金及現金等價物
       Cash and short-term deposits in the statement of                  綜合財務狀況報表的現金及現金
       financial position comprise cash at banks and on                  等價物包括銀行現金及手頭現金，
       hand and short-term highly liquid deposits with a                 以及於三個月或之內到期的短期
       maturity of three months or less, that are held for the           高 流 動 性 存 款，乃 持 作 應 付 短 期
       purpose of meeting short-term cash commitments                    現 金 承 諾，並 可 隨 時 轉 換 為 已 知
       and are readily convertible to a known amount of                  數 額 現 金、承 受 較 低 價 值 變 動 風
       cash and subject to an insignificant risk of changes in           險。
       value.

       For the purpose of the consolidated statement of                  就 綜 合 現 金 流 量 報 表 而 言，現 金
       cash flows, cash and cash equivalents consist of                  及現金等價物包括現金及短期存
       cash and short-term deposits, as defined above,                   款（定義見上文），扣除未償還銀
       net of outstanding bank overdrafts as they are                    行 透 支，此 乃 由 於 有 關 金 額 被 視
       considered an integral part of the Group’s cash                   為本集團現金管理的組成部分。
       management.

                A provision is recognised when a present obligation            如因過往事件產生現有責任（法定

                amount recognised for a provision is the present               認的金額為履行責任預期所需的

                value amount arising from the passage of time is

   (u) Revenue recognition                                           (u) 收益確認
       Revenue from contracts with customers                              客戶合同收入

       Revenue from contracts with customers is                           客戶合同收入於商品或服務的控
       recognised when control of goods or services is                    制 權 轉 讓 予 客 戶 時 確 認。該 金 額
       transferred to the customers at an amount that                     能反映本集團預期就交換該等商
       reflects the consideration to which the Group                      品或服務有權獲得的代價。
       expects to be entitled in exchange for those goods
       or services.

       When the consideration in a contract includes a                    當合同中的代價包含可變金額時，
       variable amount, the amount of consideration is                    代價金額估計為本集團向客戶轉
       estimated to be that which the Group will be entitled              讓商品或服務而有權收取的金額。
       in exchange for transferring the goods or services to              可變代價於合同開始時估計並受
       the customer. The variable consideration is estimated              到 約 束，直 至 與 可 變 代 價 相 關 的
       at contract inception and constrained until it is highly           不 確 定 因 素 其 後 得 到 解 決 時，確
       probable that a significant revenue reversal in the                認的累積收益金額極有可能不會
       amount of cumulative revenue recognised will not                   發生重大收益撥回。
       occur when the associated uncertainty with the
       variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

       When the contract contains a financing component                   當合同包含為向客戶轉讓商品或
       which provides the customer with a significant                     服務而向客戶提供超過一年的重
       benefit of financing the transfer of goods or services             大 融 資 利 益 的 融 資 成 分 時，收 入
       to the customer for more than one year, revenue                    以 應 收 款 項 的 現 值 計 量，並 採 用
       is measured at the present value of the amount                     合同開始時體現在本集團與客戶
       receivable, discounted using the discount rate                     之間的單獨融資交易中的貼現率
       that would be reflected in a separate financing                    進 行 折 現。當 合 同 包 含 為 本 集 團
       transaction between the Group and the customer                     提供超過一年的重大財務利益的
       at contract inception. When the contract contains a                融 資 成 分 時，根 據 合 同 確 認 的 收
       financing component which provides the Group with                  入包括根據實際利率法計入合約
       a significant financial benefit for more than one year,            負 債 的 利 息 開 支。對 於 客 戶 付 款
       revenue recognised under the contract includes the                 至承諾的商品或服務的轉移之間
       interest expense accreted on the contract liability                的 期 限 為 一 年 或 更 短 的 合 同，則
       under the effective interest method. For a contract                採用國際財務報告準則第 15 號中
       where the period between the payment by the                        的 實 際 權 宜 法，不 因 應 重 大 融 資
       customer and the transfer of the promised goods or                 成分的影響對交易價格進行調整。
       services is one year or less, the transaction price is
       not adjusted for the effects of a significant financing
       component, using the practical expedient in IFRS 15.

                      in the future.

                (c)   The costs are expected to be recovered.                 (c)   有關成本預期可收回。

                The capitalised contract costs are amortised and              資本化的合約成本按與資產相關
                charged to the statement of profit or loss on a               的商品或服務轉移至客戶一致的
                systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer         系 統 方 式 攤 銷 並 於 損 益 表 扣 除。
                to the customer of the goods or services to which             其他合約成本在發生時列為支出。
                the asset relates. Other contract costs are expensed
                as incurred.

  recoverable. An impairment exists when the carrying value          超 出 其 可 收 回 金 額（即 其 使 用 價 值 與
  of an asset or a CGU exceeds its recoverable amount,               公允價值減去處置成本兩者之較高者）
  which is the higher of its VIU and FVLCOD. Further details         時，即出現減值。進一步詳情載於財務
  are given in note 15 to the financial statements.                  報表附註 15。

  Estimation of defined benefit obligations                          估計界定福利責任

  The present value of the defined benefit obligations               界定福利責任的現值取決於多項因素，
  depends on a number of factors that are determined on              而有關因素使用多項假設按精算基準

  amount of pension obligations. Details of key assumptions          主要假設可能出現的變動的影響於附

           owners with lease periods of 50 and 60 years, and no                   60 年 的 租 賃 土 地，且 根 據 這 些 土 地 的
           ongoing payments will be made under the terms of these                 租約條款，無後續將付款項。房產租賃

      31. DEFINED BENEFIT PLAN OBLIGATIONS                               31. 界定福利計劃責任（續）
           (continued)

           The remeasurement of the defined benefit obligations            界定福利責任的重新計量主要指由於
           represent mainly actuarial losses arising from changes          人 口 假 設、財 務 假 設 和 經 驗 調 整 的 變

41. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                       41. 財務風險管理目標及政策
    OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
  The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise                 本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現
  cash and cash equivalents, restricted and pledged bank               金等價物、限制性和已抵押銀行存款、
  deposits, other financial assets at amortised cost, financial        其 他 按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量 的 金 融 資 產、以
  assets at FVTPL and interest-bearing bank borrowings.                公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金
  The main purpose of these financial instruments is to                融 資 產 以 及 計 息 銀 行 借 款。這 些 金 融
  finance the Group’s operations. The Group has various                工具的主要目的是為本集團的運營提
  other financial assets and liabilities such as receivables           供 資 金。本 集 團 還 有 其 他 各 種 金 融 資
  from IOT hospitals, trade and bills receivables, financial           產和負債，例如來自 IOT 醫院之應收款
  assets included in prepayments, deposits and other                   項、應收貿易款項及應收票據、計入預
  receivables, balances with related parties, trade and bills          付 款 項、保 證 金 及 其 他 應 收 款 項 的 金

   Capital Management                                                資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management          本集團資金管理的主要目標為確保本
   are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going       集 團 持 續 經 營 能 力，維 持 穩 健 資 本 比
   concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to        率，以支持業務並盡量擴大股東價值。
   support its business and maximise shareholders’ value.

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes                 本 集 團 管 理 資 本 結 構，並 視 乎 經 濟 狀
   adjustments to it in light of changes in economic                 況的變動及相關資產的風險特徵作出
   conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying         調整。為維持或調整資本結構，本集團
